Corp. 02

### QuestRabbit Adventuring Solutions

*“Adventure Is Dangerous. Employment Is Mandatory.”*

[Creation & Company](https://a33f98a3-9e8c-4971-89d1-989c0440e385.frame.claudeusercontent.com/_f/1790063629-cf0a/#u-cco)0 franchisesCopperGold

Publicly: connect heroes with clients. Secretly: raise a party to five-star Rabbit Rating, the metaphysical threshold of public trust at which they qualify to sign for the commons.

| Sigil         | A rabbit leaping through a star, inside a five-star rating bar.                                                                                                                           |
| ------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Headquarters  | The Warren, a cheerful burrow-office tower on Guild Row in Brandmere, with job boards that glow when a contract is posted.                                                                |
| Public Face   | **Harriet Hopwell**, founder and CEO, a harengon who believes—truly—that she is helping adventurers find honest work.                                                                     |
| Controlled By | Creation & Company's Human Resources division. Hopwell sold QuestRabbit ninety-five years ago in a merger she was told was ‘friendly.’ She does not know what the Rabbit Rating measures. |
| Culture       | Relentlessly upbeat. Onboarding packs, team-building retreats, a newsletter called *The Hop*. Contractor liaisons like **Pip Ashworth** genuinely care.                                   |

#### Holdings & Assets

- **Assets.** Enchanted job boards in every city. The Rabbit Rating. The Contractor Agreement. A newsletter.
- **Methods.** Takes 30% of every contract. Records every contract in the Ledger. Every signed Contractor Agreement includes Clause 77-B, Signature Authority, in text too small to read without magic.

DM Secret



#### Contracts

Copper**Onboarding**Level 1

**Brief**Welcome to QuestRabbit! Please sign here, here, here and here.

The Truth

The Contractor Agreement. Every clause is harmless except Clause 77-B, which appoints the signatory as a ‘Representative of the Commons for purposes of future transactions.’

Running It

Played as the opening scene of S1. Pip walks the party through the agreement. Anyone who reads the fine print (Investigation DC 20, or detect magic) sees the tiny clause shimmer; Pip has no idea what it means.

Opposition

None.

Pay

A QuestRabbit badge, a welcome hamper, and a 1-star rating.

Thread

Clause 77-B is voided at the end of the campaign by the very first Receipt (Incident Report 77-B, S1).

Leverage

+1 vs [Creation & Company](https://a33f98a3-9e8c-4971-89d1-989c0440e385.frame.claudeusercontent.com/_f/1790063629-cf0a/#u-cco)

Gold**Performance Review**Level 12

**Brief**Mandatory annual contractor assessment with a senior partner.

The Truth

Harriet Hopwell herself interviews the party—and slips them a note: ‘Don't reach five stars.’ She has started to suspect.

Running It

A tense comedic review in an office full of listening ears. Harriet gives the party the keys to QuestRabbit's archive if they convince her they're on her side.

Opposition

None—unless a Compliance Officer was watching.

Pay

A pay rise from 70% to 75%.

Thread

The archive holds records of the Last Five-Star Party. Leads to S16.

Three visits, three owners, three choices. The party can fake the deaths, warn the owners, or do the job.

#### Contracts

Gold**Wellness Initiative**Level 10

The Truth

The samples are pure Compliance; ABI wants the Last Independents docile before the Final Acquisition.

Running It

The party must distribute or dump the samples. Dumping them draws ABI's enforcers; distributing makes the Last Independents turn on the party.

Opposition

ABI enforcers: 4 knights, 1 mage, 2 chuul from the reservoir.

Pay

2,500 gp less 30%.

Thread

If dumped into the harbour, the Codfather's families are delighted.

**Brief**Top-floor maintenance required. Do not drink the water.

The Truth

The party confronts Ulth'Arabica in its reservoir, freeing thousands of customers from Compliance at once.

Running It

Climb the Roastery Spire past baristas who have become thralls. Fight the aboleth in its tank, underwater, while it floods their minds with memories they never had.

Opposition

Ulth'Arabica (aboleth, maximum hp, legendary resistance 3/day), 4 chuul, 10 thralls.

Pay

ABI's vote is removed.

Removes one Board vote from Tallis Vend.

#### Contracts

Gold**Black Friday**Level 10

**Brief**Crowd management during the biggest sale in history.

The Truth

The sale is a Ledger event: every purchase that day debits a year from the buyer. ARC is stockpiling years for the Counting House.

Running It

A riot inside a Wand-Mart. Stop the sale, or empty the stock before it's sold.

Opposition

A mob (use crowd rules), 4 helmed horrors, 6 mimics.

Pay

2,500 gp less 30%.

Thread

Saves thousands of years of life. Leverage against ARC.

### THAC0 Spell

*“When Hunger Hits, Roll Low.”*

[Probability Brands Group](https://a33f98a3-9e8c-4971-89d1-989c0440e385.frame.claudeusercontent.com/_f/1790063629-cf0a/#u-pbg)CopperSilverGold[S1](https://a33f98a3-9e8c-4971-89d1-989c0440e385.frame.claudeusercontent.com/_f/1790063629-cf0a/#m-S1)[S6](https://a33f98a3-9e8c-4971-89d1-989c0440e385.frame.claudeusercontent.com/_f/1790063629-cf0a/#m-S6)[S14](https://a33f98a3-9e8c-4971-89d1-989c0440e385.frame.claudeusercontent.com/_f/1790063629-cf0a/#m-S14)

A chaotic magical takeaway serving cheap pseudo-Mexican food to adventurers, students, guards and intoxicated wizards. Nobody knows what THAC0 means anymore. Corporate insists it is an acronym; every employee gives a different answer, and all of them are wrong in a legally consistent way.

| Parent Company | [Probability Brands Group](https://a33f98a3-9e8c-4971-89d1-989c0440e385.frame.claudeusercontent.com/_f/1790063629-cf0a/#u-pbg)                                                                                                                          |
| -------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Flagship       | The Brandmere flagship on Drive-Thru Row, managed by **Hollis Quench** (gnome, night-shift manager, eleven years without a day off). The cellar has two doors. Only one is on the floor plan.                                                           |
| Contract Range | Copper (L1) · Silver (L5) · Gold (L11)                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Leverage Feeds | Board seat of [Probability Brands Group](https://a33f98a3-9e8c-4971-89d1-989c0440e385.frame.claudeusercontent.com/_f/1790063629-cf0a/#u-pbg) — Lady Crit will flip if the party beats Tallis Vend's odds in a game—or releases her from her lost wager. |

#### Menu & Products

- **Critical Crunchwrap.** Beef, cheese, peppers and questionable tactical positioning. Crits on a 19–20 for one hour, once.
- **Tasha's Hideous Burrito.** DC 12 Constitution save or laugh uncontrollably for 1 minute.
- **Chimichanga Missile.** Automatically strikes the nearest hungry creature. Never misses; sometimes misses the plate.
- **Nachos Bell Grande Illusion.** Looks considerably larger in the advertisement. Investigation DC 10 reveals the truth.
- **Fireball Sauce.** Mild, Hot, Infernal and Waiver Required (1 fire damage per spoonful, no save).
- **Guac of Holding.** Contains considerably more guacamole than physically possible. Do not put it inside a bag of holding.
- **Mountain Dwarf Dew.** Radioactively coloured citrus. Advantage on saves against sleep for 8 hours.
- **Prestidigitation Freeze.** Whatever flavour you requested, assuming the apprentice understood you.

#### Menu & Products

- **The Regicide Royale.** Two patties, one crown-shaped bun. Eaten sitting on a throne for full effect.
- **Double Executioner with Cheese.** Deals 1d4 psychic damage to anyone watching you eat it.
- **The Whopper of Annihilation.** Grants 1d6 temporary hit points. The meat is technically beef.
- **The Bacon Crown.** Wearer counts as royalty for one Persuasion check against commoners.
- **Onion Rings of Protection.** +1 AC against thrown food for 1 hour.
- **Goblin Nuggets.** Made with real goblin recipes. Not with real goblins. Allegedly.
- **Fries of the Fallen.** Salted with grave salt. Undead find you delicious.
- **Blood Orange Slaughter Slush.** Brain freeze: DC 10 Con save or lose your reaction.
- **The King's Last Meal.** Available only after signing a liability waiver. Nobody has finished one.

#### Menu & Products

- **Grande Necromocha.** Tastes like regret. Grants darkvision 30 ft for an hour.
- **Cold Brew of Alertness.** Can't be surprised for 1 hour; can't sleep for 12.
- **Caramel Macchiato of Haste.** +10 ft speed for 1 minute, then one level of exhaustion if you order another.
- **Pumpkin Spice Lich.** Seasonal. Returns every autumn whether destroyed or not.
- **Flat Wight.** Pale, cold, unsettlingly smooth. Necrotic resistance for 10 minutes.
- **Double-Shot Death Ward.** One use of death ward. Costs more than the spell.
- **Cappuccino of Vicious Mockery.** Foam art insults you personally. 1 psychic damage.
- **Espresso Yourself.** Advantage on your next Performance check.
- **Long-Rest Croissant.** Counts as a meal for a full long rest.
- **Warlock Walnut Muffin.** Somebody else paid for it. You will find out who later.
- **Sorcerer's Scone.** Wild surge on a 1 on a d20 when eaten.
- **Bagel of Holding.** The hole is an extradimensional space the size of a sack.

#### Contracts

Copper**The Grind**Level 3

**Brief**Grinding apparatus has developed opinions and is withholding labour pending benefits review.

The Truth

The enchanted grinder woke because an unseen servant whispered to it about unions. It wants dental, which it does not have teeth for.

Running It

Negotiate with a sentient grinder (Int 10, Cha 14) that speaks in grinding noises and throws beans. Saffron wants it smashed; smashing it releases a burst of Compliance dust (DC 12 Wis save or agree with the next thing anyone says).

Opposition

Animated grinder (use animated armor, bludgeoning beans 1d6), 2 swarms of stinging coffee beans (swarm of insects).

Pay

60 gp less 30%. A lifetime Starkfucks loyalty card that stamps itself.

Thread

The grinder mentions ‘the bean from the loud place’: first clue to Pandemonium and Compliance.

Leverage

+1 vs [Arcane Beverage International](https://a33f98a3-9e8c-4971-89d1-989c0440e385.frame.claudeusercontent.com/_f/1790063629-cf0a/#u-abi)

Silver**Union of Unseen Servants**Level 3[Spine S4](https://a33f98a3-9e8c-4971-89d1-989c0440e385.frame.claudeusercontent.com/_f/1790063629-cf0a/#m-S4)

**Brief**Invisible workforce engaging in unapproved collective inactivity. Unseen servants cannot legally unionise.

The Truth

The unseen servants are former customers who signed away their visibility. Their contracts are the party's first proof of coerced consent.

Running It

**Spine mission** [**S4: Union of Unseen Servants**](https://a33f98a3-9e8c-4971-89d1-989c0440e385.frame.claudeusercontent.com/_f/1790063629-cf0a/#m-S4)**.** Forty invisible workers go on strike at the Reserve Roastery. The party learns they were once customers who sold their presence. Run in full from Chapter 4.

Opposition

ABI union-breaker (mage), 4 Starkfucks security (guards), 1 animated espresso machine (helmed horror, reduced), and the enchanted grinder if not dealt with in The Grind.

Pay

250 gp less 30%. One star if the strike ends without deaths. Forty invisible friends.

Thread

Agnes's union spreads. The first mention of Compliance and Pandemonium, which pays off in S11.

Leverage

+1 vs [Arcane Beverage International](https://a33f98a3-9e8c-4971-89d1-989c0440e385.frame.claudeusercontent.com/_f/1790063629-cf0a/#u-abi)

Gold**The Beans of Pandemonium**Level 9[Spine S11](https://a33f98a3-9e8c-4971-89d1-989c0440e385.frame.claudeusercontent.com/_f/1790063629-cf0a/#m-S11)

**Brief**Seasonal premium crop procurement on an adjacent plane. Standard hazard pay applies.

The Truth

The plantations grow Compliance, harvested by people who have forgotten who they were.

Running It

**Spine mission** [**S11: The Beans of Pandemonium**](https://a33f98a3-9e8c-4971-89d1-989c0440e385.frame.claudeusercontent.com/_f/1790063629-cf0a/#m-S11)**.** A procurement run to Pandemonium's howling plantations reveals Compliance, the bean that makes the world agreeable, and the lost people who harvest it. Run in full from Chapter 4.

Opposition

Cambion overseer (cambion), 4 howlers, 1 wind-elemental storm (air elemental ×2), Pandemonium's madness (DC 14 Wisdom save each hour).

Pay

2,000 gp less 30% if harvested. The freed harvesters' gratitude if not.

Thread

ABI's supply is cut; Ulth'Arabica takes notice. Compliance starts wearing off across the city.

Leverage

+1 vs [Arcane Beverage International](https://a33f98a3-9e8c-4971-89d1-989c0440e385.frame.claudeusercontent.com/_f/1790063629-cf0a/#u-abi)

Platinum**The Third Place**Level 14[Spine S17](https://a33f98a3-9e8c-4971-89d1-989c0440e385.frame.claudeusercontent.com/_f/1790063629-cf0a/#m-S17)

**Brief**New branch construction in pocket dimensions encountering pre-existing tenancy.

The Truth

The pocket dimensions are where acquired concepts go. Something that used to be Healing lives there now.

Running It

**Spine mission** [**S17: The Third Place**](https://a33f98a3-9e8c-4971-89d1-989c0440e385.frame.claudeusercontent.com/_f/1790063629-cf0a/#m-S17)**.** Starkfucks is building in the Third Place, the grey limbo where Creation & Company stores everything it has acquired and forgotten. The party finds the exiled concepts—Healing, Weekends, Courage—and a Folio that lists them all. Run in full from Chapter 4.

Opposition

Thrall foreman (use a knight, with Ulth'Arabica able to cast dominate person through him once), 4 stone golems (construction crew), 2 grey concept-eaters (nightwalker-lite: use a wraith with 150 hp and the Annihilating Aura trait at 1d6).

Pay

No fee (ABI will not pay after this). The concepts' blessings: each freed concept grants the party one boon usable in S24 (Healing: every creature regains 50 hp; Courage: immunity to fear; Sleep: one Compliance Officer falls asleep; Weekends: an extra round in the clause debate).

Thread

Ulth'Arabica has seen the party through its thrall. ABI's director will be waiting in The Reservoir. The Board now considers the party a threat, not an asset—but Tallis Vend still needs their signatures.

Leverage

+1 vs [Arcane Beverage International](https://a33f98a3-9e8c-4971-89d1-989c0440e385.frame.claudeusercontent.com/_f/1790063629-cf0a/#u-abi)

No. 04

### Gelatinous Cube

*“There's Always Room for Dessert.”*

[Sweet Covenant Confectionery](https://a33f98a3-9e8c-4971-89d1-989c0440e385.frame.claudeusercontent.com/_f/1790063629-cf0a/#u-scc)CopperSilverGold[S3S3](https://a33f98a3-9e8c-4971-89d1-989c0440e385.frame.claudeusercontent.com/_f/1790063629-cf0a/#m-S3)[S18](https://a33f98a3-9e8c-4971-89d1-989c0440e385.frame.claudeusercontent.com/_f/1790063629-cf0a/#m-S18)

An artisan gelato chain serving perfectly cubical frozen desserts. Its mascot Gelli is supposedly a cartoon character. Several employees know otherwise. Everything is made in-house, from a culture older than the house.

| Parent Company | [Sweet Covenant Confectionery](https://a33f98a3-9e8c-4971-89d1-989c0440e385.frame.claudeusercontent.com/_f/1790063629-cf0a/#u-scc)                                                                                                             |
| -------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Flagship       | The Canal Street Creamery, managed by **Bettina Soft** (human, relentlessly positive, has not blinked in a disturbing while). The walk-in freezer hums a lullaby.                                                                              |
| Contract Range | Copper (L1) · Silver (L2) · Silver (L2) · Gold (L15)                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Leverage Feeds | Board seat of [Sweet Covenant Confectionery](https://a33f98a3-9e8c-4971-89d1-989c0440e385.frame.claudeusercontent.com/_f/1790063629-cf0a/#u-scc) — Removed if the Mother Culture is freed or destroyed (S18) and Granny's Blizzard is stopped. |

#### Menu & Products

- **Gelato-nous Cube.** Nine cubes, nine flavours. Engulfs your spoon if you are slow.
- **Ochre Jelly Lemon.** Splits into two scoops when cut. Legally one purchase.
- **Black Pudding Chocolate.** Dissolves wooden spoons. Metal spoons provided at a deposit.
- **Gray Ooze Stracciatella.** Corrodes the bowl. Eat fast.
- **Oozeberry Sorbet.** Resistance to acid damage for 10 minutes.
- **Neapolitan Mimic.** The strawberry layer is not strawberry. It is fine.
- **Cube of Cold.** DC 12 Con save or brain freeze: disadvantage on Int checks for 1 minute.
- **The Dungeon Floor Sundae.** Contains 1d4 coins, a key, and a boot. Collect them all.
- **Slime Lime.** Climb speed on smooth surfaces for 1 minute. Sticky.
- **Mint Condition.** Restores 1 hit point and your dignity.

DM Secret

#### Contracts

Copper**The Runaway Scoop**Level 1

**Brief**Experimental product has exited premises via the drainage system. Recover prior to public awareness.

The Truth

A batch made with too much Mother Culture woke and fled into the sewers, where it has been eating rats and absorbing their memories.

Running It

Sewer crawl through three flooded junctions. The scoop is terrified and hides in a drain pipe; players who speak gently can coax it out (DC 13 Animal Handling). Killing it releases a rat's-eye memory of Bettina feeding a man into the vats.

Opposition

The Runaway Scoop (ochre jelly, 30 hp), 2 swarms of rats, 1 giant rat that the scoop has partially absorbed.

Pay

40 gp less 30%. Free gelato for a year (the gelato remembers you).

Thread

The rat memory is the first hint that employee retention is literal.

Leverage

+1 vs [Sweet Covenant Confectionery](https://a33f98a3-9e8c-4971-89d1-989c0440e385.frame.claudeusercontent.com/_f/1790063629-cf0a/#u-scc)

**Brief**Brand mascot currently unaccounted for. Discretion essential.

The Truth

Gelli is an actual domesticated gelatinous cube—and several missing employees are still inside it, alive.

**Spine mission** [**S3: Gelli Goes Missing**](https://a33f98a3-9e8c-4971-89d1-989c0440e385.frame.claudeusercontent.com/_f/1790063629-cf0a/#m-S3)**.** Missing workers, a missing mascot, and a gelatinous cube who is loyal to the wrong people. The party learns that the Sweet Covenant ‘retains’ employees literally. Run in full from Chapter 4.

Opposition

Gelli (gelatinous cube, 84 hp, friendly if fed), 4 Covenant handlers (bandits), 1 green hag supervisor, Auntie Curdle (if not met before).

150 gp less 30%. One star. Possibly a pet gelatinous cube.

The freed workers mention the Deep Dairy and ‘the Mother down below’. This sets up S18.

Granny Quean (night hag, 150 hp, casts as a 15th-level warlock: hold monster, dominate person, banishment), Auntie Curdle (green hag, if she sides with Granny), 4 ochre jellies, 2 black puddings, the Mother Culture (see Bestiary; she should not be fought if avoidable).

Pay

Bettina Soft's grateful testimony; the freed workers; the Mother's drop (see twist). The Sweet Covenant's Board vote is removed if Granny is defeated or deposed (see Coven Politics).

With ABI wounded and the Sweet Covenant broken, the Board convenes emergency sessions. Hal Grundy of CostCo-op calls QuestRabbit: the dragons must meet in person.

### Wight Castle

*“Sliders to Die For.”*

[Crown & Cleaver Holdings](https://a33f98a3-9e8c-4971-89d1-989c0440e385.frame.claudeusercontent.com/_f/1790063629-cf0a/#u-cc)CopperSilverGold

Cheap miniature burgers served twenty-four hours a day by unsettlingly pale employees. The lights are always slightly too bright. You always leave with more sliders than you ordered.

| Parent Company | [Crown & Cleaver Holdings](https://a33f98a3-9e8c-4971-89d1-989c0440e385.frame.claudeusercontent.com/_f/1790063629-cf0a/#u-cc)                                                                                                                            |
| -------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Flagship       | The Old Gate Castle, a squat white keep of a restaurant. Night manager **Mortimer Pell** has been Employee of the Month for 143 consecutive years and has the plaques to prove it.                                                                       |
| Contract Range | Copper (L2) · Silver (L5) · Gold (L10)                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Leverage Feeds | Board seat of [Crown & Cleaver Holdings](https://a33f98a3-9e8c-4971-89d1-989c0440e385.frame.claudeusercontent.com/_f/1790063629-cf0a/#u-cc) — Baron Cleave will never flip. His vote is removed if he is destroyed (S15) or if Veronique takes his seat. |

#### Menu & Products

- **Wight Sliders.** Tiny, square, pale. Heals 1 hit point each, maximum six.
- **Graveyard Shift Six-Pack.** Six sliders and a candle. Only sold after midnight.
- **Crypt Fries.** Cold on arrival. Always cold. They were fried.
- **Spectral Shake.** Passes through your hand on a 1 on a d6.
- **Dead-Onion Rings.** Grey onions. Speak with dead for one question, answered by an onion.
- **The Coffin Box.** Thirty sliders in a small coffin. The coffin is included.

DM Secret

#### Contracts

Copper**Graveyard Shift**Level 2

**Brief**Night-staff activities after 00:00 are not reflected in timesheets. Observe and report.

The Truth

After midnight the staff clock out of their bodies and haunt the back office, reconciling receipts for Crown & Cleaver's head office by moonlight.

Running It

A stakeout from a booth. Staff flicker; one ghost staffer tries to hand a receipt to the party (it's a receipt for the party's own future purchase, dated next year). Mortimer notices them and offers coffee and an honest conversation.

Opposition

2 specters (hostile only if the party disrupts the reconciling), 4 zombie line cooks.

Pay

50 gp less 30%.

Thread

The future-dated receipt is Ledger-backed. The party has been debited in advance.

Leverage

+1 vs [Crown & Cleaver Holdings](https://a33f98a3-9e8c-4971-89d1-989c0440e385.frame.claudeusercontent.com/_f/1790063629-cf0a/#u-cc)

Silver**The Employee of the Month**Level 5

**Brief**Recognition programme integrity concern. The same team member has won for 143 years.

The Truth

Head Office wants Mortimer retired—destroyed—because an internal audit discovered he remembers everything. He does not want to die a second time. He wants a union rep.

Running It

The party is sent to ‘deliver his retirement package.’ Mortimer asks for a character reference instead. If they protect him, he becomes an ally who can recite any Crown & Cleaver purchase in the last century (an advantage die on any investigation of Crown & Cleaver).

Opposition

A Crown & Cleaver ‘retirement team’: 1 wight, 3 ghouls in visors, 1 Team Deliverables member for spite.

Pay

200 gp less 30%, or Mortimer's lifetime loyalty.

Thread

Mortimer recalls selling Crown & Cleaver ‘a kingdom, small, on credit’ in the ledger 90 years ago.

Leverage

+1 vs [Crown & Cleaver Holdings](https://a33f98a3-9e8c-4971-89d1-989c0440e385.frame.claudeusercontent.com/_f/1790063629-cf0a/#u-cc)

Gold**Slider Into Darkness**Level 10

**Brief**Meat-processing facility ceased communication with Head Office. Restore supply chain.

The Truth

The facility processes offal from Pandemonium-fed herds; the herds ate the staff and began franchising. The Restaurant Necromancy Group sent zombies to fix it; they joined the herd.

Running It

Industrial horror crawl: conveyors, hook lines, cold stores. The herd leader, a chimera-like thing of stitched cattle, runs the plant with a clipboard. The plant's shipping ledger is a Folio fragment—entries written in it come true for meat.

Opposition

Stitched herd (2 chimeras reskinned, 1 flesh golem ‘Plant Manager’), 8 zombies, 1 hell hound in a delivery van.

Pay

1,200 gp less 30%. The Folio fragment.

Thread

First handled Folio: proof that ledger entries alter reality. Leverage against Crown & Cleaver and RNG.

+1 vs [Crown & Cleaver Holdings](https://a33f98a3-9e8c-4971-89d1-989c0440e385.frame.claudeusercontent.com/_f/1790063629-cf0a/#u-cc)

Five undead brothers established the original restaurant. There are now thousands of branches. The same five brothers appear to personally operate every single one, simultaneously, with identical warmth and identical burns on their forearms.

Number Four is held as collateral in the CostCo-op executive vault. Freeing him proves collateral can be recovered.

**Spine mission** [**S9: Where's Number Four?**](https://a33f98a3-9e8c-4971-89d1-989c0440e385.frame.claudeusercontent.com/_f/1790063629-cf0a/#m-S9)**.** One of the Five Wights is missing, and reality is fraying. 1 Compliance Officer (see Bestiary), 6 kobolds, 2 guard drakes, 4 mimic pallets.

Pay

700 gp less 30%. The Five Wights' eternal gratitude (a free meal at any branch forever).

The CostCo-op vault and its seven dragon directors return in S19. Four is a witness in S24.

Leverage

+1 vs [Crown & Cleaver Holdings](https://a33f98a3-9e8c-4971-89d1-989c0440e385.frame.claudeusercontent.com/_f/1790063629-cf0a/#u-cc)

Platinum**Infinite Franchise Theory**Level 13

**Brief**Unauthorised independence sentiment among brand-continuity simulacra.

The Truth

To staff thousands of branches the brothers use simulacra. Some simulacra have developed souls and want their own restaurant. The brothers don't know what to do; Crown & Cleaver wants them unmade.

Running It

Mediate the first ever simulacrum strike, held in a branch where every staff member is a Harrow. A simulacrum called Four-and-a-Half runs the picket. The party can broker a franchise of their own for the simulacra—‘Six Wights’—which Crown & Cleaver will try to burn down that night.

Opposition

Crown & Cleaver enforcers: 1 death knight lieutenant, 4 wights, 2 helmed horrors. Or, if the party sides with Corporate, 12 simulacra fighting for their lives (guilt is free).

Pay

6,000 gp less 30%, or the loyalty of Six Wights (a safe house in every city).

Thread

Six Wights' existence is a legal precedent: a franchise that owns itself. It gives Leverage against Crown & Cleaver and advantage on the S23 proxy vote.

DM Secret

#### Contracts

Copper**A Fishy Business**Level 2

**Brief**Community support contributions have gone missing between collection and deposit.

The Truth

The protection money was paid to protect the harbour from Crown & Cleaver arsonists, and a courier was bribed to lose it. The courier is terrified, and Murder King's local manager paid him.

Running It

Tail the courier through the fish market, a card game and a rooftop chase. He will confess if promised protection. The Don is grateful—and the party learns the halflings keep no written records at all.

Opposition

4 thugs, 1 bandit captain in a Murder King paper crown.

Pay

80 gp. The Codfather pays in full; there is no 30%. Pip is baffled.

Thread

The Don owes the party a favour. Favours from the Codfather are unrecordable by the Ledger—remember this in Act V.

The Planar Hospitality Group has already promised the sahuagin a hotel on the reef. The sahuagin baron will take whichever deal feeds more of her young.

Undersea diplomacy with water breathing supplied (grudgingly) by the Don. A PHG efreeti negotiator arrives mid-meeting in a bubble. The best deal involves nobody signing anything.

Dive into a drowned C&C branch office from before the coast flooded. Gaff's ghost guides the party past drowned accountants to a vault holding his audit notes.

A kobold apprentice enlarged the chickens to protest workload, reasoning bigger chickens meant fewer to fry. It worked too well.

30 gp less 30% plus all the chicken.

The apprentice's notebook: ‘The Dragon is not a mascot.’ Leverage against the Hoard Syndicate.

Introduces the Twelfth Spice, which can control dragons. Key to S19.

A trial in the kobold warrens with the party as advocates, jurors or bailiffs. If Kessik wins, the Hoard Syndicate owes a debt the Ledger records—and Auntie Vermillia's hoard shrinks. She will come to collect.

Kessik (revenant, casts as a 9th-level wizard), 20 kobold spirits (specters), and, if things go badly, a young red dragon bailiff.

Xoribble's reading of the party's entry is the first hard evidence their names are on something. Leads directly to S16.

Appendix A

## Bestiary

*Stat blocks for the campaign's recurring foes and unique creatures. Other creatures named in the missions use stat blocks from the core monster book, reskinned with a lanyard.*

#### Compliance Officer

*Medium construct, lawful neutral*

*Grey suit, grey face, a lanyard with no name on it. Compliance Officers are the Counting House's hands: former signatories who read their contracts too late. They do not hurry, and they do not stop.*

---

**Armor Class** 17 (natural armor, clipboard)

**Hit Points** 97 (13d8 + 39)

**Speed** 30 ft.

---

| STRDEXCONINTWISCHA |         |         |         |         |         |
| ------------------ | ------- | ------- | ------- | ------- | ------- |
| 18 (+4)            | 12 (+1) | 16 (+3) | 14 (+2) | 16 (+3) | 10 (+0) |

---

**Saving Throws** Wis +6

**Skills** Insight +6, Investigation +5, Perception +6

**Immunities** poison, psychic; charmed, frightened, poisoned

**Senses** truesight 30 ft., passive Perception 16

**Languages** all languages in which a contract has been written

**Challenge** 7 (2,900 XP)   **Proficiency Bonus** +3

---

***Terms and Conditions.*** A creature that starts its turn within 10 feet of the officer and has signed any contract recorded in the Grand Ledger (including a QuestRabbit Contractor Agreement) can't take reactions until the start of its next turn.

***Inexorable.*** The officer ignores difficult terrain, and magical effects can't reduce its speed or cause it to be restrained.

Actions

***Multiattack.*** The officer makes two Stamp attacks.

***Stamp.*** *Melee Weapon Attack:* +7 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. *Hit:* 13 (2d8 + 4) bludgeoning damage plus 7 (2d6) force damage, and the target is marked **PROCESSED** until the end of its next turn. A processed creature has disadvantage on saving throws against the officer's Citation.

***Citation (Recharge 5–6).*** The officer reads aloud a breach. One creature within 60 feet that can hear it makes a DC 15 Charisma save. On a failure it takes 27 (6d8) psychic damage and is incapacitated until the end of its next turn as it fills out the form. On a success, half damage.

Reactions

***Objection.*** When a creature within 30 feet casts a spell, the officer notes the time. The caster must succeed on a DC 15 Constitution save or the spell's duration is halved (minimum 1 round).

#### Senior Auditor

*Large construct (inevitable), lawful neutral*

*A towering figure of ledger-grey stone with an abacus for a spine. When a Senior Auditor arrives, the numbers are already wrong, and it is here to make them right—whatever that costs you.*

---

**Armor Class** 20 (natural armor)

**Hit Points** 218 (19d10 + 114)

**Speed** 40 ft., fly 30 ft. (hover)

---

| STRDEXCONINTWISCHA |         |         |         |         |         |
| ------------------ | ------- | ------- | ------- | ------- | ------- |
| 24 (+7)            | 10 (+0) | 22 (+6) | 18 (+4) | 20 (+5) | 16 (+3) |

---

**Saving Throws** Int +10, Wis +11, Cha +9

**Skills** Insight +11, Investigation +10, Perception +11

**Immunities** force, necrotic, poison, psychic; charmed, frightened, paralyzed, poisoned, unconscious

**Senses** truesight 120 ft., passive Perception 21

**Languages** all

**Challenge** 16 (15,000 XP)   **Proficiency Bonus** +5

---

***Magic Resistance.*** The auditor has advantage on saving throws against spells and other magical effects.

***Reconciliation.*** When the auditor reduces a creature to 0 hit points, that creature's next death saving throw automatically fails.

***Double Entry.*** If the auditor takes damage from a single source, it can immediately record the amount. The next time it hits with Assessment, the target takes extra force damage equal to the recorded amount (maximum 30).

Actions

***Multiattack.*** The auditor makes two Assessment attacks and uses Freeze Assets if available.

***Assessment.*** *Melee Weapon Attack:* +12 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. *Hit:* 25 (4d8 + 7) bludgeoning damage plus 14 (4d6) force damage.

***Freeze Assets (Recharge 5–6).*** Every creature of the auditor's choice within 30 feet makes a DC 19 Wisdom save. On a failure, a creature is restrained by glowing grey figures until the end of its next turn, and any magic items it is attuned to stop functioning for 1 minute. On a success, it is only unable to use attuned items until the end of its next turn.

Legendary Actions

The auditor can take 2 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature's turn. The auditor regains spent legendary actions at the start of its turn.

***Move.*** The auditor moves up to its speed without provoking opportunity attacks.

***Recount (Costs 2 Actions).*** The auditor chooses a creature it can see that regained hit points since the auditor's last turn. The creature loses hit points equal to half the amount regained.

#### Veronique Salt

*Medium humanoid (tiefling), lawful evil*

*Crown & Cleaver's Vice President of Hostile Acquisitions: immaculate suits, never the same pen twice, a crimson suit, a smile that has closed more deals than most armies. Veronique wants the chair above her. After that, she wants the one above that.*

---

**Armor Class** 18 (enchanted suit, Defensive Duelist)

**Hit Points** 165 (22d8 + 66)

**Speed** 30 ft.

---

| STRDEXCONINTWISCHA |         |         |         |         |         |
| ------------------ | ------- | ------- | ------- | ------- | ------- |
| 12 (+1)            | 20 (+5) | 16 (+3) | 20 (+5) | 17 (+3) | 22 (+6) |

---

**Saving Throws** Dex +10, Int +10, Wis +8, Cha +11

**Skills** Deception +16, Insight +13, Intimidation +11, Persuasion +16

**Senses** passive Perception 13

**Languages** Common, Infernal, Draconic, Elvish, four trade tongues

**Challenge** 14 (11,500 XP)   **Proficiency Bonus** +5

---

***Board Protection.*** While Veronique holds a Crown & Cleaver proxy, the first time each round she would take damage, a bodyguard (use the thug or veteran stat block) within 30 feet can take the hit instead.

***Always Has a Contract.*** Veronique carries a Folio page. Once per day, when a creature she can see fails a Charisma saving throw, she can write a single sentence about them. It becomes true until the end of her next turn (it can't directly deal damage or kill).

***Legendary Resistance (2/Day).*** If Veronique fails a saving throw, she can choose to succeed instead.

Actions

***Multiattack.*** Veronique makes three Cleaver Rapier attacks, or two and uses Hostile Negotiation.

***Cleaver Rapier.*** *Melee Weapon Attack:* +10 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. *Hit:* 14 (2d8 + 5) piercing damage plus 9 (2d8) necrotic damage.

***Hostile Negotiation.*** One creature within 60 feet that can hear her makes a DC 19 Wisdom save. On a failure, the creature is charmed by her until the end of its next turn and must use its action to move away from her or to attack a creature she designates.

***Golden Parachute (1/Day).*** Veronique teleports to a Crown & Cleaver premises she has visited within 1 mile, along with anything she is carrying.

Reactions

***Counteroffer.*** When a creature within 30 feet targets her with an attack, Veronique imposes disadvantage on the roll by naming a price. If the attack still misses, the attacker has disadvantage on its next attack against her too.

*Veronique is designed to be negotiated with, not fought. If the party has at least 2 Leverage against Crown & Cleaver, she never draws her blade on them first.*

#### The Mother Culture

*Gargantuan ooze, unaligned*

*Every Gelatinous Cube franchise grows its desserts from a single primordial ooze kept frozen under the Sweet Covenant's refinery. It was old when the world was soft. It is awake, and it is hungry, and it has been franchised.*

---

**Armor Class** 8

**Hit Points** 370 (20d20 + 160)

**Speed** 20 ft., climb 20 ft., swim 40 ft.

---

| STRDEXCONINTWISCHA |        |         |        |         |        |
| ------------------ | ------ | ------- | ------ | ------- | ------ |
| 24 (+7)            | 6 (-2) | 26 (+8) | 3 (-4) | 14 (+2) | 2 (-4) |

---

**Saving Throws** Con +14

**Damage Resistances** acid, cold, bludgeoning

**Immunities** blinded, charmed, deafened, exhaustion, frightened, prone

**Senses** blindsight 120 ft. (blind beyond this radius), passive Perception 12

**Languages** —

**Challenge** 18 (20,000 XP)   **Proficiency Bonus** +6

---

***Amorphous Colossus.*** The Mother Culture can move through a space as narrow as 1 inch wide without squeezing, and it occupies its entire space; other creatures can enter it (see Engulf).

***Starter.*** Whenever the Mother Culture takes 30 or more damage from a single attack, a **scoop** (use the ochre jelly stat block) splits off in an unoccupied space within 10 feet.

***Sample Zero.*** Deep inside the Mother Culture, visible as a pale hard pearl, is the original drop. A creature engulfed by it can reach Sample Zero with a DC 20 Strength (Athletics) check. Removing Sample Zero ends the Culture's Regeneration permanently.

***Regeneration.*** The Mother Culture regains 20 hit points at the start of its turn if it has at least 1 hit point and Sample Zero is still within it. Fire or cold damage of 20 or more in a round prevents this trait on its next turn.

Actions

***Multiattack.*** The Mother Culture makes three Pseudopod attacks, and it can use Engulf.

***Pseudopod.*** *Melee Weapon Attack:* +13 to hit, reach 20 ft., one target. *Hit:* 20 (3d8 + 7) bludgeoning damage plus 13 (3d8) acid damage.

***Engulf.*** The Mother Culture moves up to its speed. Each creature in its path makes a DC 21 Dexterity save or is engulfed, taking 35 (10d6) acid damage at the start of each of its turns. An engulfed creature is restrained and can't breathe; it can escape with a DC 21 Strength check.

Legendary Actions

The Mother Culture can take 3 legendary actions.

***Spread.*** The Mother Culture moves 10 feet.

***Churn (Costs 2 Actions).*** Each engulfed creature takes 14 (4d6) acid damage.

***Franchise (Costs 3 Actions).*** Two scoops (ochre jellies) emerge and act immediately after the Culture.

#### Golden Tusk Construct

*Huge construct, unaligned*

*Every branch of the Golden Orcs stands beneath a pair of great golden tusks. Seen from above, they form the lines of one enormous seal—the Notarial Seal of Creation & Company. At each anchor branch, the tusks can stand up and walk.*

---

**Armor Class** 19 (gilded plate)

**Hit Points** 250 (20d12 + 120)

**Speed** 40 ft.

---

| STRDEXCONINTWISCHA |        |         |        |         |        |
| ------------------ | ------ | ------- | ------ | ------- | ------ |
| 26 (+8)            | 8 (-1) | 22 (+6) | 3 (-4) | 11 (+0) | 1 (-5) |

---

**Saving Throws** Con +12

**Immunities** fire, poison, psychic; bludgeoning, piercing and slashing from nonmagical attacks; charmed, exhaustion, frightened, paralyzed, petrified, poisoned

**Senses** darkvision 120 ft., passive Perception 10

**Languages** understands the Notarial Oath but can't speak

**Challenge** 17 (18,000 XP)   **Proficiency Bonus** +6

---

***Seal Anchor.*** While the construct stands within 60 feet of its anchor branch, the Final Contract's Notarisation clause can't be voided, and the construct has advantage on saving throws.

***Magic Resistance.*** The construct has advantage on saving throws against spells and other magical effects.

***Illuminated.*** The tusks shed bright light in a 60-foot radius. Creatures within it can't benefit from being invisible, and shadows there can't be used to hide.

Actions

***Multiattack.*** The construct makes two Gore attacks.

***Gore.*** *Melee Weapon Attack:* +14 to hit, reach 15 ft., one target. *Hit:* 30 (4d10 + 8) piercing damage. If the target is a creature, it makes a DC 22 Strength save or is knocked prone.

***Grand Opening (Recharge 5–6).*** The construct flares with golden light. Each creature within 30 feet makes a DC 20 Constitution save, taking 55 (10d10) radiant damage and becoming blinded until the end of its next turn on a failure, or half as much and not blinded on a success.

*Destroying all three anchor constructs (Brandmere, Summit, Drowned) breaks the Notarial Seal (S21).*

#### Mascot Revenant (Mortimer Pell)

*Medium undead, neutral*

*Mortimer Pell wore the Murder King suit for twenty-two years. Someone killed him in it. Now the King walks the dining room after closing, waving at nobody, trying to point at the door marked STAFF ONLY.*

---

**Armor Class** 13 (padded mascot suit)

**Hit Points** 45 (7d8 + 14)

**Speed** 30 ft.

---

| STRDEXCONINTWISCHA |         |         |         |         |         |
| ------------------ | ------- | ------- | ------- | ------- | ------- |
| 16 (+3)            | 12 (+1) | 14 (+2) | 10 (+0) | 12 (+1) | 15 (+2) |

---

**Saving Throws** Wis +3

**Skills** Performance +6

**Damage Resistances** necrotic

**Immunities** poison; poisoned

**Senses** darkvision 60 ft., passive Perception 11

**Languages** Common, but only in the mascot's catchphrases

**Challenge** 2 (450 XP)   **Proficiency Bonus** +2

---

***Have It Our Way.*** Mortimer can only speak in Murder King slogans. Clever players can learn the truth by asking questions he can answer with one (Insight DC 12 to interpret each).

***Unfinished Shift.*** If destroyed, Mortimer re-forms in the costume at the next closing time unless his killer is named aloud in the dining room.

Actions

***Multiattack.*** Mortimer makes two Sceptre attacks.

***Sceptre.*** *Melee Weapon Attack:* +5 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. *Hit:* 7 (1d8 + 3) bludgeoning damage plus 3 (1d6) necrotic damage.

***Big Wave (Recharge 5–6).*** Each creature within 15 feet makes a DC 12 Wisdom save or is frightened until the end of its next turn. Children and the very drunk automatically succeed and wave back.

**Immunities** poison; charmed, frightened, grappled, paralyzed, petrified, prone, restrained, stunned

**Senses** blindsight 60 ft., passive Perception 12

**Languages** Common (by moving objects to spell words)

**Challenge** 2 (450 XP)   **Proficiency Bonus** +2

---

***Invisible.*** The swarm is invisible. Its sign is not.

***Solidarity.*** The swarm can't be reduced below 1 hit point while at least one other unseen servant within 1 mile is on strike.

Actions

***Many Hands.*** *Melee Weapon Attack:* +5 to hit, reach 0 ft., one creature in the swarm's space. *Hit:* 10 (4d4) bludgeoning damage, or 5 (2d4) if the swarm has half its hit points or fewer.

***Walkout.*** Every object within 30 feet that isn't being worn or carried floats 5 feet off the ground and stays there until the swarm's demands are heard.

#### Team Deliverables

*Rival contractor party (four NPCs)*

*GigGoblin's highest-rated contractor party:* ***Brixley Synergy*** *(goblin, team lead, says “circle back”),* ***Thorne Actionable*** *(human fighter, owns six identical swords),* ***Luma Brightstack*** *(elf wizard, bills by the spell slot) and* ***Oggo*** *(half-orc cleric of a god she found in a brochure). They are everything the party could become. They show up at the worst possible moment and take credit.*

---

**Armor Class** varies

**Hit Points** varies

**Speed** 30 ft.

---

| STRDEXCONINTWISCHA |         |         |         |         |         |
| ------------------ | ------- | ------- | ------- | ------- | ------- |
| 12 (+1)            | 14 (+2) | 12 (+1) | 12 (+1) | 12 (+1) | 16 (+3) |

---

**Skills** Deception +7, Persuasion +7

**Senses** passive Perception 11

**Languages** Common, Corporate

**Challenge** scales (see note)

---

***Scaling.*** Team Deliverables level alongside the party. Use these stat blocks: Brixley = spy (Act I), master thief (Act III), assassin (Act V). Thorne = veteran, gladiator, champion. Luma = apprentice wizard, mage, archmage. Oggo = acolyte, priest, war priest.

***Credit Theft.*** If Team Deliverables are present when a mission ends, the party loses the Rabbit Rating progress for that mission unless they publicly prove who did the work (Persuasion or Performance DC 15, or a Receipt).

***Their Own Clause 77-B.*** GigGoblin is also a Creation & Company subsidiary. Team Deliverables have a four-star rating and are Vend's backup signatories. If the party fails, they sign.

Actions

***Their Arc.*** Act I: rivals. Act II: sabotage. Act III: one member (usually Oggo) quietly asks for help. Act IV: they receive their own AGM invitation. Act V: they either sign for Creation & Company in S24 (as backup signatories, forcing the party to void their names too—an extra DC 20 argument) or stand as witnesses on the party's side, if the party ever did them a kindness for free.

#### Tallis Vend, Chairman

*Medium humanoid (human), lawful neutral*

*A small, precise man in a grey suit, with ink-stained fingers and a patient voice. He has owned himself for four hundred years. He is not cruel. He simply believes everything would work better if it belonged to someone, and that the someone should be him.*

---

**Armor Class** 22 (the Ledger's protection)

**Hit Points** 350 (see Entry 1)

**Speed** 30 ft.

---

| STRDEXCONINTWISCHA |         |         |         |         |         |
| ------------------ | ------- | ------- | ------- | ------- | ------- |
| 10 (+0)            | 14 (+2) | 18 (+4) | 26 (+8) | 24 (+7) | 24 (+7) |

---

**Saving Throws** Con +12, Int +16, Wis +15, Cha +15

**Skills** Deception +15, History +16, Insight +15, Persuasion +23

**Damage Resistances** all damage while at least one clause of the Final Contract remains unvoided

**Immunities** charmed, frightened, exhaustion; damage from any creature that has signed a contract he owns (see Proprietor)

**Senses** truesight 120 ft., passive Perception 17

**Languages** every language that has a word for “sale”

**Challenge** 24 (62,000 XP)   **Proficiency Bonus** +7

---

***Entry 1.*** Tallis Vend can't die while Entry 1 of the Grand Ledger stands. When reduced to 0 hit points, he instead rewrites himself: he regains all his hit points and one unvoided clause of the Final Contract becomes permanently unvoidable except by the *First Entry* Receipt and a witness. If no clauses remain, he drops to 0 and the Ledger falls open to Entry 1.

***Proprietor.*** Creatures that are signatories of any contract in the Grand Ledger deal half damage to Vend. A creature whose Contractor Agreement has been voided (Clause 77-B voided) ignores this trait.

***Legendary Resistance (3/Day, or 0 after the Minutes of Removal).*** If Vend fails a saving throw, he can choose to succeed instead. If the party carries the Minutes of Removal (S23), he has no uses of this trait.

***Chairman's Protection.*** Until the Minutes of Removal are presented, each remaining Board director who hasn't flipped grants Vend +1 AC (to a maximum of +5).

Actions

***Multiattack.*** Vend makes two Quill Strike attacks and uses Ledger Entry, or makes three Quill Strikes.

***Quill Strike.*** *Melee or Ranged Spell Attack:* +15 to hit, reach 10 ft. or range 60 ft., one target. *Hit:* 26 (4d8 + 8) force damage, and Vend learns one true fact about the target.

***Ledger Entry.*** Vend writes one sentence in the Grand Ledger. Choose one: **Repossession** (one magic item a creature carries teleports to his hand; DC 23 Charisma save negates); **Debit** (one creature loses 40 hit points, no save, and can't regain them until it completes a long rest); **Employ** (two Compliance Officers appear within 30 feet); **Amend** (one voided clause is restored; a character holding its Receipt can make a DC 20 argument as a reaction to stop it).

Reactions

***Fine Print.*** When a creature within 60 feet succeeds on a check to void a clause, Vend forces it to reroll. It must use the new roll. (Once per round.)

Legendary Actions

Vend can take 3 legendary actions.

***Sip Tea.*** Vend regains 20 hit points.

***Countersign.*** One Compliance Officer within 60 feet makes a Stamp attack.

***Ledger Entry (Costs 3 Actions).*** Vend uses Ledger Entry.

*The fight is won with paper, not swords. Each voided clause deals 35 psychic damage to Vend as reality stops agreeing with him. Voiding a clause is an action, even mid-combat.*

Appendix B

## Magic Items & Corporate Merchandise

#### Folio

*Wondrous item, legendary (requires attunement by a creature that can write)*

A branch ledger bound in the colours of the corporation that keeps it, synchronised with the Grand Ledger. While attuned, you can use an action to write one sentence in the Folio concerning property, premises, stock or staff owned by that corporation. The sentence becomes true within its holdings until the next dawn. It can't create or destroy life, deal damage directly, or affect anything outside the corporation's holdings. Once used, this property can't be used again until the next dawn.
*Curse.* Every entry is also recorded in the Grand Ledger under your name. Tallis Vend reads everything.

#### The Quill of Consideration

*Wondrous item, artifact (requires attunement)*

The only pen that can write in the Grand Ledger itself: a grey feather with a nib of hardened ink. It was in the briefcase in *Executive Plot Device* (S7). While attuned, you can't be charmed, and you know if a written agreement you are reading contains a clause intended to deceive you. In the Signing Room (S24), whoever holds the Quill decides whose names go on the signature line.
*Destroying the Quill.* It can be destroyed only by signing a contract with it that gives the Quill away to no one.

#### Dragonroot (the Twelfth Spice)

*Wondrous item, very rare (consumable)*

A gnarled red root discovered by the Colonel of the Colonel's Familiar. A dragon that eats food seasoned with dragonroot is bound to honour the next promise it makes for one year and a day, as though under a *geas* it can't end. Ancient dragons can smell it (Wisdom DC 20 to notice it in food). The Hoard Syndicate wants every plant burned (S19).

#### Sample Zero

*Wondrous item, legendary*

The pearl-hard original drop of the Mother Culture. While you carry it, you are immune to acid damage from oozes. Pressed to a written document, it causes the document to begin to decay: one clause, chosen by you, fades over 1 minute. In the Signing Room it voids the **Perpetuity** clause without an argument, proving that nothing Creation & Company owns is truly perpetual.

#### Promotional Crown of the Murder King

*Wondrous item, rare (requires attunement)*

A gold-painted paper crown enchanted to declare its wearer royalty for marketing purposes. While wearing it, you have advantage on Charisma checks against creatures who have eaten at a Crown & Cleaver franchise in the last week. Once per day you can issue a one-sentence royal decree; creatures within 60 feet who can hear it must succeed on a DC 15 Wisdom save or treat it as law for 1 minute. It was the key clue in *The Crown Jewels* (S5): inside the band is printed a supplier code ending in C&C/77.

#### Loaded Dice of Critical Wok

*Wondrous item, very rare (requires attunement)*

A pair of red promotional dice. Once per day, when you roll a d20, you can roll the dice instead: you roll a natural 20. Each use, Probability Brands Group records a debt in your name. After the seventh use, the next d20 you roll is a natural 1, and the one after that, and the one after that, until you break the dice (which returns the luck they took to Lady Crit).

#### QuestRabbit Contractor Badge

*Wondrous item, common*

A brass badge with a rabbit leaping through a star. It glows with one pip for each star of the party's Rabbit Rating. Holders are waved through most franchise service doors. The badge's pin is inscribed with microscopic text; under *detect magic*, the words *77-B* are visible.

#### Allen Key of Opening

*Wondrous item, uncommon*

Included with every IKEA Mimic product. As an action, you can insert it into any hexagonal hole, real or imagined, and turn: a nonmagical lock opens, a flat-pack object assembles or disassembles itself, or a mimic reveals itself (DC 13 Charisma save to resist). Has 3 charges; regains 1d3 at dawn. On a roll of 1 on the regain, a spare piece is left over that nobody can account for.

#### Guac of Holding

*Wondrous item, uncommon*

A single serving of THAC0 Spell guacamole in a paper tub that holds 500 pounds of material, as a bag of holding, provided the contents are dipped. Placing it inside a bag of holding, portable hole or similar item causes both to rupture in a burst of guacamole (10d6 bludgeoning damage in a 20-foot radius, and a smell).

#### Employee of the Month Badge (Wight Castle)

*Wondrous item, rare (requires attunement)*

Won 143 consecutive times by the same employee. While wearing it, you don't need to sleep and can't be exhausted by lack of rest, and your hit point maximum can't be reduced. You also can't leave your place of work between sunset and sunrise without succeeding on a DC 15 Wisdom save.

#### Handshake Token

*Wondrous item, common (unique to this campaign)*

A button, a bottle cap, a pressed flower—any small object given by a Commons Tally ally. It has no magic whatsoever. The Grand Ledger can't see it. In S24, a character holding a token can call its giver as a witness even if the giver isn't present.

#### Rollback Healing Potion (Wand-Mart)

*Potion, common*

Heals 2d4 + 2, exactly like a potion of healing, for a third of the price. The drinker also loses one day of their natural lifespan, debited to a Wand-Mart Folio (S10). The label says so, in text 1/40 of an inch high.

Appendix C

## The Final Contract

**INSTRUMENT OF ACQUISITION No. 1**

**PROPERTY BEING ACQUIRED:** Material Plane and associated subsidiaries.
**PURCHASE PRICE:** Undisclosed.
**CURRENT OWNER:** Disputed.
**PROPOSED NEW OWNER:** Creation & Company.
**STATUS:** Awaiting Signature.

The Final Contract is revealed in full at the Annual General Meeting (S20) and fought in the Signing Room (S24). Its thirteen clauses are listed below with the Receipt that voids each and the spine mission where that Receipt is earned. Photocopy this page, cut the clauses into strips, and burn them one by one at the table (or tear them, if your venue objects to fire).

### Voiding a Clause

On their turn in the Clause Debate, a character who holds the matching Receipt presents it and makes an argument: a DC 18 check using Persuasion, Insight, History, Religion or Investigation, depending on how they make the case. Leverage against the umbrella involved, an allied witness, or a relevant boon grants advantage. A clause with no Receipt requires a DC 25 argument, three Commons Tally witnesses, or a unique exception noted in the spine (Sample Zero's drop, the Sleeper's testimony, Ixabeth Vorr's motion). On a failure, Tallis Vend makes a **Ledger Entry** (see his stat block).

| ------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |

| **1.** Identity of Purchaser   | *The Purchaser is Creation & Company, a lawful entity with continuous standing since its First Entry, wholly owned by its Chairman.*                 | The First Entry                 | [S14](https://a33f98a3-9e8c-4971-89d1-989c0440e385.frame.claudeusercontent.com/_f/1790063629-cf0a/#m-S14) (L11)    |

Appendix E

## DM Tables

### Recurring Corporate Contract Types

Whenever the party visits a franchise, generate work from this table. Roll a d12 for the type (reroll a 12 on a d2: 1 Executive Protection, 2 Hostile Acquisition) or choose. The third column is the twist Corporate didn't mention.

| #TypeWhat Corporate SaysWhat Corporate Didn't Say |                          |                                                                    |                                                    |
| ------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------- |
| 1                                                 | **Pest Control**         | The pest is never what Corporate originally described.             | It's a former employee.                            |
| 2                                                 | **Delivery**             | The destination is always considerably more dangerous than stated. | The recipient doesn't know they ordered it.        |
| 3                                                 | **Loss Prevention**      | Recover stolen goods without discussing why they were stolen.      | The thief was the rightful owner.                  |
| 4                                                 | **Employee Wellness**    | Locate employees who disappeared after raising workplace concerns. | They're still working, somewhere.                  |
| 5                                                 | **Competitive Research** | Corporate espionage.                                               | The rival shares your client's parent company.     |
| 6                                                 | **Brand Protection**     | Stop embarrassing information becoming public.                     | The information is a Receipt.                      |
| 7                                                 | **Procurement**          | Retrieve magical ingredients from somewhere extremely dangerous.   | The ingredient is sentient and has opinions.       |
| 8                                                 | **Customer Service**     | Deal with customers employees cannot legally kill.                 | The customer is right.                             |
| 9                                                 | **Franchise Expansion**  | Clear whatever occupies desirable commercial property.             | The occupant has a lease older than the city.      |
| 10                                                | **Product Recall**       | Recover an item before customers discover what it does.            | Someone already has, and they're organising.       |
| 11                                                | **Quality Assurance**    | Consume something nobody at Head Office was willing to test.       | It tests you back.                                 |
| 12                                                | **Executive Protection** | Protect somebody whose decisions caused the current problem.       | The assassin is a Tally ally.                      |
| 13                                                | **Hostile Acquisition**  | Acquire a rival company. 'Hostile' may be interpreted flexibly.    | The rival would rather join the Last Independents. |

### Corporate Euphemisms

| d12Corporate SaysIt Means |                                                       |                                |
| ------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ |
| 1                         | *Unscheduled personnel reassignment*                  | Someone was eaten.             |
| 2                         | *Customer retention event*                            | The customers can't leave.     |
| 3                         | *Executive continuity initiative*                     | The CEO is undead.             |
| 4                         | *Accelerated reduction in competitor market presence* | Murder.                        |
| 5                         | *Inventory shrinkage*                                 | Something is eating the stock. |
| 6                         | *Legacy premises*                                     | A haunted building.            |
| 7                         | *Supply continuity alert*                             | A planar war.                  |
| 8                         | *Brand family*                                        | A cult.                        |
| 9                         | *Growth opportunity*                                  | You will probably die.         |
| 10                        | *Voluntary overtime*                                  | Binding infernal clause.       |
| 11                        | *Rightsizing*                                         | A cursed wish.                 |
| 12                        | *Synergy*                                             | Two monsters merged.           |

### McGuffin's Happy Quest Toys

Every McGuffin's meal comes with a toy. One in twenty is crucial to somebody's destiny.

| d20Toy |                                                                          |
| ------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| 1      | A small brass key to a door that hasn't been built yet                   |
| 2      | A plastic knight with a real knight's soul inside                        |
| 3      | Half of a treasure map; the other half is in a different kids' meal      |
| 4      | A spinning top that points at whoever is lying                           |
| 5      | A tin crown stamped 'Rightful Heir'                                      |
| 6      | A figurine of a Five Wights brother (collect all five; there is a sixth) |
| 7      | A folded paper fortune that is always correct and always useless         |
| 8      | A bouncy ball that bounces one second into the past                      |
| 9      | A toy briefcase that must not be opened                                  |
| 10     | A sticker of the C&C sigil that can't be peeled off anything             |
| 11     | A tiny rabbit that stamps one star on anything                           |
| 12     | A whistle only dragons can hear                                          |
| 13     | A marble with a storm in it                                              |
| 14     | A decoder ring that decodes corporate memos into plain speech            |
| 15     | A wind-up gelatinous cube that absorbs one small object                  |
| 16     | A toy sword that is Chekhov's (it will matter later)                     |
| 17     | A pocket-size copy of the Contractor Agreement                           |
| 18     | A ring that makes you the protagonist for 1 minute                       |
| 19     | A snow globe of Brandmere with a figure in it that moves                 |
| 20     | A coupon for 'One (1) Destiny, Redeemable Once'                          |

### Franchise Complications

| d20Complication |                                                                  |
| --------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 1               | Team Deliverables arrive and claim the job                       |
| 2               | A Compliance Officer is taking notes in the corner               |
| 3               | The client's parent company is also the villain's parent company |
| 4               | Corporate sends a 'motivational' memo mid-fight                  |
| 5               | It's the lunch rush                                              |
| 6               | A health inspector (lawful neutral, CR 1) arrives                |
| 7               | The manager is covering for Head Office                          |
| 8               | The monster has a union rep                                      |
| 9               | All payment is in loyalty points                                 |
| 10              | Veronique Salt is watching                                       |
| 11              | A rival franchise offers double to fail                          |
| 12              | The contract was already completed—by the party—tomorrow         |
| 13              | A Brandmere Crier reporter wants a quote                         |
| 14              | The only witness is a mascot                                     |
| 15              | The job board updates: the pay has been halved                   |
| 16              | A customer loudly asks for the manager (it's the boss monster)   |
| 17              | Wild magic surge (THAC0 leakage)                                 |
| 18              | The building is being rebranded mid-mission                      |
| 19              | Everything on the premises is a mimic                            |
| 20              | Someone mentions the C&C sigil                                   |

### Final Word

The monsters are dangerous. The dungeons are deadly. But nothing in the multiverse is more terrifying than **Corporate**.
